FS-03 Distinct feat bonuses and retained comparison families NEW joint convention: after eligibility, flat numeric bonuses from DISTINCT feat identities add unless an explicit shared comparison family says otherwise. FS-04 Native damage, percentages and hostile slows NEW percentage convention: each separately eligible percentage feat computes its own floored increment from the SAME native packet, after actual critical-dice adjustment and its ordinary native ability/flat formula but BEFORE external riders, other percentage increments, saves and defenses. A qualifying mental-effect save is the effect's own save to prevent or end Charmed, Frightened, possession, compelled actions/speech, or an explicitly described alteration of thoughts, memories, beliefs, emotions or perceived sensory reality; also include a save directly against psychic damage.
